The translated content is then re-anchored to the original layout through an adaptive typesetting engine. Experiments on a curated 200-page benchmark, together with human evaluation and multimodal LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, show that BabelDOC improves layout fidelity, visual aesthetics, and terminology consistency over representative baselines, while maintaining competitive translation precision. The open-source toolkit and its interactive downstream applications are publicly available and have attracted over 8.4K GitHub stars and 17 contributors at the time of writing. A demonstration video is also available.

</details>


### [151] [Probing Routing-Conditional Calibration in Attention-Residual Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09850)
*Wenhao Liang,Lin Yue,Wei Emma Zhang,Miao Xu,Mingyu Guo,Olaf Maennel,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力残差（AR）Transformer中，路由追踪信息能否在置信度之外提供稳定的后验校准信号。通过匹配置信度的诊断套件、核回归探针、以及控制容量的MLP对比实验，发现路由摘要并未提供稳定的校准改善，表面提升经匹配置信度、容量、置换检验等控制后消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管路由增强型架构常伴随样本级别的内部路由追踪，并声称这些信息与不确定性校准相关，但目前的后验校准方法通常仅基于logits或softmax置信度。作者希望探究路由追踪是否真的能为后验校准提供超越置信度的稳定额外信号。

Method: 在Attention-Residual Transformer上构建匹配置信度的诊断套件：按路由状态分层、计算子组差异并与路由排列零模型对比；评估仅辅助特征不同的匹配后验探针；使用AR-CondCal（2维Nadaraya-Watson探针）并对比置信度仅含和预测熵控制；进行带宽敏感性检查；使用全向量MLP并控制容量匹配的置信度仅含MLP和打乱路由轮廓。

Result: 标量路由摘要未提供稳定的路由条件校准证据：加权间隙小且对种子敏感；30个箱内排列检验中仅1个在α=0.05下拒绝条件零假设（且仅在一个种子上，不稳定）；AR-CondCal的种子方差带与对照模型重叠，未可靠改善最差路由三分位ECE；全向量MLP的改进在控制容量匹配的置信度仅含MLP后消失，打乱路由轮廓也能达到类似性能。

Conclusion: 在AR设置中，表面上的路由感知校准提升不应被解读为内部状态校准，直到匹配置信度、带宽、容量和排列控制排除了常见的混淆因素。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration is usually evaluated as a function of logits or softmax confidence alone, even as routing-augmented architectures increasingly accompany predictions with sample-specific internal routing traces and pair them with claims of calibration-relevant uncertainty. We ask a basic question: do these traces provide stable routing-specific evidence for post-hoc calibration beyond confidence? We study this in Attention-Residual transformers (Kimi Team, 2026) through a matched-confidence diagnostic suite that stratifies examples by routing-derived state, compares subgroup gaps against within-bin routing-permutation nulls, and evaluates matched post-hoc probes differing only in their auxiliary feature. Across our completed AR runs, scalar routing summaries do not provide stable evidence of routing-conditional miscalibration: weighted gaps remain small or seed-sensitive, and only $1$ of $30$ within-bin permutation tests rejects the conditional-null at $α=0.05$ (only on one seed; not stable across seeds in that cell). AR-CondCal, a minimal $2$-D Nadaraya--Watson probe on confidence and routing-depth variance, lies within the seed-variance band of matched confidence-only and predictive-entropy controls and does not reliably improve worst-routing-tertile ECE; bandwidth-sensitivity checks (Scott multiples, CV-NLL, global-ECE oracle) do not change this. A full-vector MLP over $(c, H_1, \ldots, H_L)$ can appear to improve over a linear confidence baseline, but the apparent gain disappears once a capacity-matched confidence-only MLP is included as a control, and shuffled routing profiles achieve comparable performance. Apparent routing-aware calibration gains in this AR setting should not be read as internal-state calibration until matched-confidence, bandwidth, capacity, and permutation controls rule out common confounds.

</details>


### [152] [MoPO: Incorporating Motion Prior for Occluded Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09856)
*Tao Tang,Hong Liu,Xinshun Wang,Wanruo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoPO方法，利用运动先验（pose序列）来恢复遮挡下的人体网格，包含运动去遮挡模块和运动感知融合细化模块，在遮挡和标准基准上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体网格恢复方法在遮挡场景下鲁棒性不足，由于遮挡部位缺乏空间特征，常导致姿态不准确和严重的运动抖动。

Method: MoPO包含两个核心模块：1）运动去遮挡模块，使用时序遮挡检测器检测关节可见性，并通过轻量运动预测器基于历史姿态补全遮挡关节位置；2）运动感知融合与细化模块，将补全后的关节序列与图像特征融合以估计人体形状和初始姿态，再通过逆向运动学利用补全的关节序列细化最终人体姿态。

Result: 在遮挡相关和标准基准上均达到最先进性能，显著提升了遮挡场景下人体网格恢复的准确性和时序一致性。

Conclusion: 利用运动序列先验可以有效提升遮挡下的人体网格恢复效果，MoPO验证了运动先验对遮挡部位估计的有效性。

Abstract: Although recent studies have made remarkable progress in human mesh recovery, they still exhibit limited robustness to occlusions and often produce inaccurate poses and severe motion jitter due to the insufficient spatial features for occluded body parts. Inspired by the rapid advancements in human motion prediction, we discover that compared to occluded image features, pose sequence inherently contains reliable motion prior for estimating occluded body parts. In this paper, we incorporate Motion Prior for Occluded human mesh recovery, called MoPO. Our MoPO mainly consists of two components: 1) The motion de-occlusion module, where we propose a spatial-temporal occlusion detector to detect joint visibility, and then we propose a lightweight motion predictor to complete the occluded body parts by predicting the most plausible joint positions based on history poses. 2) The motion-aware fusion and refinement module, which fuses the completed joint sequence with image features to estimate human shape and initial human pose. Moreover, the completed joint sequence is further used to refine the final human pose through inverse kinematics, which provides the occlusion-free motion prior for regressing human poses. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MoPO achieves state-of-the-art performance on both occlusion-specific and standard benchmarks, significantly enhancing the accuracy and temporal consistency of occluded human mesh recovery. Our code and demo can be found in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [153] [Clip-level Uncertainty and Temporal-aware Active Learning for End-to-End Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09858)
*Riku Inoue,Shogo Sato,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Tomoyasu Shimada,Toshihiko Nishimura,Ryuichi Tanida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CUTAL方法，针对端到端MOT模型进行片段级主动学习，在仅用50%标注数据时达到与全监督相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习方法在帧级别运行，与依赖多帧片段的现代端到端追踪器的训练推理结构不匹配，导致标注效率低下。

Method: 提出片段级主动学习框架CUTAL，利用多帧预测的不确定性度量捕捉帧间对应歧义，并结合时间多样性选择信息丰富且不冗余的片段子集。

Result: 在MeMOTR和SambaMOTR上，CUTAL在相同标注预算下优于基线方法；仅用50%标注数据即在MeMOTR上达到与全监督相当的性能。

Conclusion: 片段级主动学习更适配端到端MOT模型，CUTAL有效提升了标注效率并降低了标注成本。

Abstract: Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) in dynamic environments relies on robust temporal reasoning to maintain consistent object identities over time. Transformer-based end-to-end MOT models achieve strong performance by explicitly modeling temporal dependencies, yet training them requires extensive bounding-box and identity annotations. Given the high labeling cost and strong redundancy in videos, Active Learning (AL) is an effective approach to improve annotation efficiency. However, existing AL methods for MOT primarily operate at the frame level, which is structurally misaligned with modern end-to-end trackers whose inference and training rely on multi-frame clips. To bridge this gap, we formulate clip-level active learning and propose Clip-level Uncertainty and Temporal-aware Active Learning (CUTAL). In contrast to frame-based approaches, CUTAL scores each clip using uncertainty metrics derived from multi-frame predictions to capture inter-frame correspondence ambiguities, while enforcing temporal diversity to select an informative and non-redundant subset. Experiments show that CUTAL achieves stronger overall performance than baselines at the same label budgets across MeMOTR and SambaMOTR. Notably, CUTAL achieves performance comparable to full supervision for MeMOTR on both datasets using only 50% of the labeled training data.

</details>


### [154] [Learning to Align Generative Appearance Priors for Fine-grained Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09859)
*Shijie Wang,Yadan Luo,Zijian Wang,Xin Yu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAPan方法，通过可逆归一化流将检索特征映射到潜密度空间，用类别条件高斯先验建模，再反向生成外观感知锚点进行对齐，提升细粒度图像检索在未见类别上的泛化能力，达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统细粒度图像检索依赖可见类别的监督，导致模型偏向可见类别的语义而忽略跨类别泛化的外观特征，限制了在未见类别上的检索性能。

Method: 1) 使用可逆归一化流将实例特征映射到潜密度空间；2) 每个可见类别用类别条件高斯先验建模，通过精确似然估计优化；3) 反向从先验高密度区采样，映射回特征空间生成外观感知锚点；4) 用先验驱动的对齐目标优化检索嵌入。

Result: 在细粒度和粗粒度基准数据集上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过将学习目标从类别预测转向外观建模，利用归一化流的可逆性保留外观细节，并引入先验驱动对齐，有效提升了模型对未见类别的泛化能力。

Abstract: Fine-grained image retrieval (FGIR) typically relies on supervision from seen categories to learn discriminative embeddings for retrieving unseen categories. However, such supervision often biases retrieval models toward the semantics of seen categories rather than the underlying appearance characteristics that generalize across categories, thereby limiting retrieval performance on unseen categories. To tackle this, we propose GAPan, a Generative Appearance Prior alignment network that reformulates the learning objective from category prediction toward appearance modeling. Technically, GAPan treats retrieval features with an invertible density model based on normalizing flows. In the forward direction, the flow maps all instance features into a latent density space, where each seen category is modeled by a class-conditional Gaussian prior and optimized via exact likelihood estimation. This formulation preserves richer appearance details by leveraging the invertible property of the flows. In the reverse direction, samples from the high-density regions of these learned priors are mapped back to the feature space to produce appearance-aware anchors that reflect intra-category variation. These anchors supervise a prior-driven alignment objective that aligns retrieval embeddings with category-specific appearance distributions, thereby improving generalization to unseen categories. Evaluations demonstrate that our GAPan achieves state-of-the-art performance on both widely-used fine- and coarse-grained benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [DA-SegFormer: Damage-Aware Semantic Segmentation for Fine-Grained Disaster Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09864)
*Kevin Zhu,William Tang,Raphael Hay Tene,Zesheng Liu,Nhut Le,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DA-SegFormer，通过类感知采样、在线难例挖掘与Dice Loss、分辨率保持推理等策略，在灾害无人机图像细粒度损伤分割任务上取得显著提升，尤其是对小样本损伤类别效果突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害后快速准确的损伤评估对应急响应至关重要，但无人机图像中细粒度损伤级别（如区分轻微与严重屋顶损伤）因图像缩放时纹理线索退化和极端类别不平衡而极具挑战性。

Method: 1) 类感知采样策略保证罕见损伤特征的暴露；2) 集成在线难例挖掘与Dice Loss动态聚焦于低表示类别；3) 采用分辨率保持推理协议保留原始纹理细节。

Result: 在RescueNet数据集上达到74.61% mIoU，较基线提升2.55%；轻微损伤类别提升11.7%，严重损伤类别提升21.3%。

Conclusion: DA-SegFormer有效解决了灾害场景下细粒度损伤分割的纹理退化与类别不平衡问题，为无人机灾害评估提供了实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Rapid and accurate damage assessment following natural disasters is critical for effective emergency response. However, identifying fine-grained damage levels (e.g., distinguishing minor from major roof damage) in UAV imagery remains challenging due to the degradation of texture cues during resizing and extreme class imbalance. We propose DA-SegFormer, a damage-aware adaptation of the SegFormer architecture optimized for high-resolution disaster imagery. Our method introduces a Class-Aware Sampling strategy to guarantee exposure to rare damage features, and it integrates Online Hard Example Mining (OHEM) with Dice Loss to dynamically focus on underrepresented classes. In addition, we employ a resolution-preserving inference protocol that maintains native texture details. Evaluated on the RescueNet dataset, DA-SegFormer achieves 74.61\% mIoU, outperforming the baseline by 2.55\%. Notably, our improvements yield double-digit gains in critical damage classes: Minor Damage (+11.7%) and Major Damage (+21.3%).

</details>


### [156] [The Cartesian Shortcut: Re-evaluate Vision Reasoning in Polar Coordinate Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09883)
*Xia Hu,Zhenrui Yue,Brian Potetz,Howard Zhou,Leonidas Guibas,Chun-Ta Lu,Zhicheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有多模态大模型在视觉推理基准中普遍存在“笛卡尔捷径”漏洞：模型利用正交网格布局可转化为文本坐标的特性，依赖文本推理而非真实视觉理解。为此提出Polaris-Bench，将53个视觉推理任务转为极坐标空间，打破正交先验。评估14个前沿模型发现，性能从笛卡尔布局的70-83%骤降至极坐标的31-39%，揭示模型缺乏拓扑不变视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在视觉推理基准上高分频现，但这些高分是否真实反映视觉理解能力存疑。作者发现模型存在利用正交网格布局中可数字化为文本坐标的“笛卡尔捷径”漏洞，亟需系统性破解。

Method: 提出Polaris-Bench基准，将53个视觉推理任务从笛卡尔坐标空间重构至极坐标空间，同时保留配对笛卡尔版本作为对照，保持逻辑约束和任务语义不变，从而彻底打破模型依赖的正交先验。

Result: 在14个前沿MLLM上评估，笛卡尔布局下性能70-83%的模型在极坐标下骤降至31-39%；即便在完全逻辑等价条件下，退化依然持续；笛卡尔布局上的推理增益在极坐标下严重削弱。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型缺乏**拓扑不变视觉推理**能力，其视觉推理表现高度依赖坐标空间的拓扑结构，而非真正的视觉理解。

Abstract: As current Multimodal Large Language Models rapidly saturate canonical visual reasoning benchmarks, a key question emerges: do these strong scores genuinely reflect robust visual understanding? We identify a pervasive vulnerability, the \textbf{Cartesian Shortcut}: visual reasoning benchmarks prevalently build on orthogonal grid-based layouts that can be readily discretized into explicit textual coordinates. Models systematically exploit this property, heavily leveraging text-based deductive reasoning to assist visual problem-solving. To systematically dismantle this shortcut, we introduce \textbf{Polaris-Bench}, which re-formulates 53 visual reasoning tasks in Polar coordinate space with paired Cartesian counterparts as reference, while preserving consistent logical constraints and task semantics -- thus fundamentally breaking the orthogonal prior that models exploit. Comprehensive evaluation across $14$ state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals that frontier models achieving $70$--$83\%$ on Cartesian layouts collapse to $31$--$39\%$ on Polar equivalents, with degradation persisting even under complete logical equivalence. Moreover, reasoning gains observed on Cartesian layouts are severely diminished on Polar equivalents. These findings expose a critical deficiency in current MLLMs: the lack of topology-invariant visual reasoning.

</details>


### [157] [Hyperbolic Distillation: Geometry-Guided Cross-Modal Transfer for Robust 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09899)
*Kanglin Ning,Wenrui Li,Houde Quan,Qifan Li,Xingtao Wang,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HGC-Det的双曲约束跨模态蒸馏方法，用于多模态3D目标检测，通过SGVO、HFT和FAGO三个核心组件解决模态异质、空间不对齐和表示危机问题，在室内外数据集上实现了检测精度与计算成本的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态蒸馏方法在点云与图像特征融合中受限于模态异质性、空间不对齐及多模态表示危机，导致效率受限。

Method: 提出HGC-Det框架，包含图像分支和点云分支；点云分支由SGVO（2D语义引导体素优化）、HFT（双曲几何约束跨模态特征传递）和FAGO（特征聚合几何优化）三个组件构成。

Result: 在SUN RGB-D、ARKitScenes、KITTI、nuScenes等室内外数据集上，该方法在检测精度与计算成本之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 双曲几何约束有效缓解了跨模态特征融合中的语义损失和表示退化问题，提升了多模态3D检测性能。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation has emerged as an effective strategy for integrating point cloud and image features in 3D perception tasks. However, the modality heterogeneity, spatial misalignment, and the representation crisis of multiple modalities often limit the efficient of these cross-modal distillation methods. To address these limitations in existing approaches, we propose a hyperbolic constrained cross-modal distillation method for multimodal 3D object detection (HGC-Det). The proposed HGC-Det framework includes an image branch and a point cloud branch to extract semantic features from two different modalities. The point cloud branch comprises three core components: a 2D semantic-guided voxel optimization component (SGVO), a hyperbolic geometry constrained cross-modal feature transfer component (HFT), and a feature aggregation-based geometry optimization component (FAGO). Specifically, the SGVO component adaptively refines the spatial representation of the 3D branch by leveraging semantic cues from the image branch, thereby mitigating the issue of inadequate representation fusion. The HFT component exploits the intrinsic geometric properties of hyperbolic space to alleviate semantic loss during the fusion of high-dimensional image features and low-dimensional point cloud features. Finally, the FAGO compensates for potential spatial feature degradation introduced by the 2D semantic-guided voxel optimization component. Extensive experiments on indoor datasets (SUN RGB-D, ARKitScenes) and outdoor datasets (KITTI, nuScenes) demonstrate that our method achieves a better trade-off between detection accuracy and computational cost.

</details>


### [158] [TOC-Bench: A Temporal Object Consistency Benchmark for Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09904)
*Junzhe Chen,Siyuan Meng,Yuxi Chen,Man Zhao,Xiaojie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TOC-Bench，一个专门评估视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）中时间对象一致性的诊断基准。通过三层时间必要性过滤协议，从17900个候选QA对中筛选出2323个人工验证的高质量样本，涵盖10个诊断维度。实验表明，当前模型在事件计数、事件排序、身份敏感推理和幻觉感知验证等方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准主要关注事件识别、动作理解或粗略的时间推理，很少评估模型在物体被遮挡、消失、重现、状态变化及跨物体交互时，能否一致地保持同一物体的身份、状态和时间连续性。这导致当前评价可能高估了模型的时间推理能力，而忽视了以物体为中心的时间连贯性失败。

Method: 提出三层时间必要性过滤协议：(1) 去除依赖语言先验的样本；(2) 去除单帧即可回答的样本；(3) 去除无序帧线索即可回答的样本。最终保留17900个时间依赖性QA对，覆盖10个诊断维度，并从中构建2323个人工验证的高质量QA对，涉及1951个视频。

Result: 三层过滤协议去除了60.7%的候选QA对。实验表明，当前Video-LLMs在时间对象一致性任务上表现不佳，尤其在事件计数、事件排序、身份敏感推理和幻觉感知验证方面存在显著弱点，尽管它们在通用视频理解基准上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 时间对象一致性是当前Video-LLMs尚未解决的主要挑战，现有模型严重依赖于粗粒度的时间理解而缺乏细粒度的物体级时间连贯性能力。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video-LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in general video understanding, yet their ability to maintain temporal object consistency remains insufficiently explored. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on event recognition, action understanding, or coarse temporal reasoning, but rarely evaluate whether a model can consistently preserve the identity, state, and temporal continuity of the same object across occlusion, disappearance, reappearance, state transitions, and cross-object interactions. As a result, current evaluations may overestimate temporal reasoning ability while overlooking failures in object-centric temporal coherence. To address this issue, we introduce TOC-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark specifically designed to evaluate temporal object consistency in Video-LLMs. TOC-Bench is explicitly object-track grounded, where each queried subject is associated with a per frame object trajectory and structured temporal event timeline. To ensure that benchmark items depend on temporally ordered visual evidence rather than language priors, single-frame shortcuts, or unordered frame cues, we propose a three-layer temporal-necessity filtering protocol that removes 60.7% of candidate QA pairs and retains 17,900 temporally dependent items spanning 10 diagnostic dimensions. From this filtered pool, we further construct a human-verified benchmark containing 2,323 high-quality QA pairs over 1,951 videos. Experiments on representative Video-LLMs show that temporal object consistency remains a major unsolved challenge. Current models exhibit substantial weaknesses in event counting, event ordering, identity-sensitive reasoning, and hallucination-aware verification, despite strong performance on general video understanding benchmarks.

</details>


### [159] [Frequency Adapter with SAM for Generalized Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09925)
*Phuoc-Nguyen Bui,Van-Nguyen Pham,Duc-Tai Le,Junghyun Bum,Hyunseung Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FSAM框架，融合LoRA高效微调和频率适配器，通过提取域不变高频特征增强SAM在医学图像分割中的域泛化能力，在眼底和前列腺数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM域泛化方法主要在空间域操作，忽略了影响模型鲁棒性的频率域差异。

Method: 提出FSAM，集成LoRA高效微调与频率适配器，提取域不变高频特征以缓解频率相关的域偏移。

Result: 在眼底和前列腺数据集上，FSAM在域泛化性能上优于传统DG方法和现有SAM-based DG方法。

Conclusion: FSAM通过引入频率域信息有效提升了SAM在医学图像分割中的域泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is a critical task in computer-aided diagnosis and treatment planning. However, deep learning models often struggle to generalize across datasets due to domain shifts arising from variations in imaging protocols, scanner types, and patient populations. Traditional domain generalization (DG) methods utilize causal feature learning, adversarial consistency, and style augmentation to improve segmentation robustness. While effective, these approaches rely on explicit feature alignment, adversarial objectives, or handcrafted augmentations, which may not fully exploit the capabilities of foundation models. Recently, the Segment Anything Model (SAM) has demonstrated strong generalization capabilities in segmentation tasks. SAM-based DG methods attempt to improve medical image segmentation. However, these approaches primarily operate in the spatial domain and overlook frequency-based discrepancies that significantly affect model robustness. In this work, we propose Frequency-based Domain Generalization with SAM (FSAM), a novel framework that integrates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for efficient fine-tuning and a frequency adapter to incorporate frequency-domain representations for single-source domain generalization. FSAM enhances SAM's segmentation robustness by extracting domain-invariant high-frequency features, mitigating frequency-related domain shifts. Experimental results on fundus and prostate datasets demonstrate that FSAM outperforms existing traditional DG and SAM-based DG approaches in domain generalization. Codes and pre-trained models will be made available on GitHub.

</details>


### [160] [Evidence-based Decision Modeling for Synthetic Face Detection with Uncertainty-driven Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09935)
*Qingchao Jiang,Zhenxuan Hou,Zhiying Zhu,Zhenxing Qian,Xinpeng Zhang,Zaiwang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EMSFD方法，利用狄利克雷分布建模类别证据并融入模型不确定性，结合不确定性驱动的主动学习策略，合成人脸检测准确率提升15%，同时增强了模型的可解释性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成人脸检测方法存在两个问题：一是依赖Softmax激活函数导致过度自信，对未知分布图像预测不可靠；二是需要大量高质量标注数据，实用性受限。

Method: EMSFD方法：1）使用狄利克雷分布建模类别证据，显式地将模型不确定性融入预测过程；2）在训练阶段利用估计的不确定性从未标注池中优先选择更具信息量的样本进行标注，降低标注成本并提升泛化能力。

Result: 与现有SOTA基线相比，准确率提升15%，增强了合成人脸检测的可解释性，展现了优越的检测性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: EMSFD通过证据建模与不确定性驱动的主动学习，有效解决了合成人脸检测中的过度自信和数据标注成本问题，显著提升了检测可靠性和泛化性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of deep generative models, forged facial images are massively exploited for illegal activities. Although existing synthetic face detection methods have achieved significant progress, they suffer from the inherent limitation of overconfidence due to their reliance on the Softmax activation function. Thus, these methods often lead to unreliable predictions when encountering unknown Out-of-Distribution (OOD) images, and cannot ascertain the model's uncertainty in its prediction. Meanwhile, most existing methods require massive high-quality annotated data, which greatly limits their practicability across diverse scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose EMSFD (Evidence-based decision Modeling for Synthetic Face Detection with uncertainty-driven active learning), an approach designed to enhance detection reliability and generalizability. Specifically, EMSFD models class evidence using the Dirichlet distribution and explicitly incorporates model uncertainty into the prediction process. Furthermore, during training, the estimated uncertainty is exploited to prioritize more informative samples from the unlabeled pool for annotation, thereby reducing labeling cost and improving model generalization. Extensive experimental evaluations demonstrate that our method enhances the interpretability of synthetic face detection. Meanwhile, our method yields a 15\% increase in accuracy compared to existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines, which demonstrates the superior detection performance and generalizability of our approach. Our code is available at: https://github.com/hzx111621/EMSFD.

</details>


### [161] [Urban-ImageNet: A Large-Scale Multi-Modal Dataset and Evaluation Framework for Urban Space Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09936)
*Yiwei Ou,Chung Ching Cheung,Jun Yang Ang,Xiaobin Ren,Ronggui Sun,Guansong Gao,Kaiqi Zhao,Manfredo Manfredini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Urban-ImageNet是一个大规模多模态数据集和评估基准，包含2019-2025年间从24个中国城市61个地点收集的200万张社交媒体图像及文本，基于城市理论构建的10类分层分类体系（HUSIC），支持场景分类、跨模态检索和实例分割三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市图像数据集缺乏理论驱动的分类体系，将城市影像简单视为通用场景数据，无法捕捉城市研究中核心的空间、社会和功能区分。作者希望构建一个基于城市理论、多城市、多模态的标准化基准，系统评估AI模型对城市空间的感知能力。

Method: 从新浪微博收集2019-2025年间24个中国城市61个地点的200万张图像及文字帖子，提出HUSIC分层分类框架（10类），构建1K/10K/100K/2M四个规模的数据子集，设计三个标准化任务：场景语义分类、跨模态图文检索、实例分割。

Result: 代表性视觉、视觉-语言和分割模型在监督场景分类上表现强劲，但在跨模态检索和实例级城市对象分割上更具挑战性。多尺度实验表明平衡训练数据从1K增至100K时模型性能持续提升。

Conclusion: Urban-ImageNet提供了一个统一的、理论驱动的、多城市的基准，用于评估AI系统在多模态、多尺度和多种任务形式下感知和理解当代城市空间的能力。数据集和基准已开源。

Abstract: We present Urban-ImageNet, a large-scale multi-modal dataset and evaluation benchmark for urban space perception from user-generated social media imagery. The corpus contains over 2 Million public social media images and paired textual posts collected from Weibo across 61 urban sites in 24 Chinese cities across 2019-2025, with controlled benchmark subsets at 1K, 10K, and 100K scale and a full 2M corpus for large-scale training and evaluation. Urban-ImageNet is organized by HUSIC, a Hierarchical Urban Space Image Classification framework that defines a 10-class taxonomy grounded in urban theory. The taxonomy is designed to distinguish activated and non-activated public spaces, exterior and interior urban environments, accommodation spaces, consumption content, portraits, and non-spatial social-media content. Rather than treating urban imagery as generic scene data, Urban-ImageNet evaluates whether machine perception models can capture spatial, social, and functional distinctions that are central to urban studies. The benchmark supports three tasks within one standardized library: (T1) urban scene semantic classification, (T2) cross-modal image-text retrieval, and (T3) instance segmentation. Our experiments evaluate representative vision, vision-language, and segmentation models, revealing strong performance on supervised scene classification but more challenging behavior in cross-modal retrieval and instance-level urban object segmentation. A multi-scale study further examines how model performance changes as balanced training data increases from 1K, 10K to 100K images. Urban-ImageNet provides a unified, theory-grounded, multi-city benchmark for evaluating how AI systems perceive and interpret contemporary urban spaces across modalities, scales, and task formulations. Dataset and benchmark are available at: huggingface.co/datasets/Yiwei-Ou/Urban-ImageNet and github.com/yiasun/dataset-2.

</details>


### [162] [SDTalk: Structured Facial Priors and Dual-Branch Motion Fields for Generalizable Gaussian Talking Head Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09956)
*Peng Jia,Zhen Xiao,Jia Li,Xueliang Liu,Zhenzhen Hu,Lingyun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SDTalk，一种基于3D高斯溅射的单次说话头合成框架，无需个性化训练即可泛化到未见身份，通过两阶段训练策略实现高质量实时合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话头合成方法依赖身份特定模型，限制了跨身份泛化能力，亟需一种无需微调即可泛化到新身份的高效方法。

Method: 两阶段训练策略：(1) 重建阶段，融入结构化面部先验，分别预测可见与遮挡区域的3DGS参数；(2) 运动阶段，引入双分支运动场分别建模粗粒度和细粒度面部动态，提升细节保真度和唇部同步。

Result: 在视觉质量和推理效率上均超越现有方法。

Conclusion: SDTalk通过3DGS与两阶段训练，有效实现了无需个性化训练的高质量实时说话头合成，为跨身份泛化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-quality, real-time talking head synthesis remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing reconstruction- and rendering-based methods typically rely on identity-specific models, limiting cross-identity generalization. To address this issue, we propose SDTalk, a one-shot 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based framework that generalizes to unseen identities without personalized training or fine-tuning. Our framework comprises two modules with a two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, we incorporate structured facial priors into the reconstruction module and separately predict 3DGS parameters for visible and occluded regions, enabling complete head reconstruction from a single image. In the second stage, we introduce a dual-branch motion field to model coarse and fine facial dynamics, improving detail fidelity and lip synchronization. Experiments demonstrate that SDTalk surpasses existing methods in both visual quality and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [163] [Learning to Perceive "Where": Spatial Pretext Tasks for Robust Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09963)
*Yang Shen,Yusen Cai,Weronika Hryniewska-Guzik,Qing Lin,Mengmi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spatial Prediction (SP) 方法，通过预测局部视图间的相对位置和尺度来学习空间结构，可无缝集成到自监督学习框架中，在多个视觉任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习主要学习物体不变性表征，忽略了物体部分之间的空间结构和关系。

Method: 提出空间预测（SP）回归任务，预测同一图像中解耦局部视图的相对位置和尺度，建模部件间的连续几何空间关系，作为可插拔模块集成到不同SSL框架中。

Result: 在图像识别、细粒度分类、语义分割、深度估计及分布外鲁棒性上均取得一致改进，并在空间推理任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: 显式建模空间信息为SSL提供有效归纳偏置，学习到更具结构性的表征和更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing self-supervised learning (SSL) methods primarily learn object-invariant representations but often neglect the spatial structure and relationships among object parts. To address this limitation, we introduce Spatial Prediction (SP), a spatially aware pretext regression task that predicts the relative position and scale between a pair of disentangled local views from the same image. By modeling part-to-part relationships in a continuous geometric space, SP encourages representations to capture fine-grained spatial dependencies beyond invariant categorical semantics, thereby learning the compositional structure of visual scenes. SP is implemented as a decoupled plug-in and can be seamlessly integrated into diverse SSL frameworks. Extensive experiments show consistent improvements across image recognition, fine-grained classification, semantic segmentation, and depth estimation, as well as substantial gains in out-of-distribution robustness for object recognition. To evaluate spatial reasoning, we introduce (1) a position and scale prediction task on image patch pairs and (2) a jigsaw understanding task requiring patch reordering and recognition after reconstruction. Strong performance on these tasks indicates improved spatial structure and geometric awareness. Overall, explicitly modeling spatial information provides an effective inductive bias for SSL, leading to more structured representations and better generalization. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [164] [OZ-TAL: Online Zero-Shot Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09976)
*Chaolei Han,Hongsong Wang,Xin Gong,Jie Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在线零样本时序动作定位（OZ-TAL）新任务，并设计了一种免训练框架，利用现成的视觉-语言模型并结合增强机制，在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-1.3上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线时序动作定位（On-TAL）方法通常在特定领域训练，泛化能力有限，难以处理任意视频中的未见动作类别。为此，本文提出在线零样本时序动作定位（OZ-TAL）任务。

Method: 提出一种免训练框架，利用现成的视觉-语言模型（VLMs），并引入额外机制增强视觉表征、减轻模型固有偏见。

Result: 在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-1.3上建立新基准，实验表明该方法在离线和在线零样本设置下均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的OZ-TAL任务和免训练框架有效解决了在线零样本动作定位问题，为未来研究提供了新方向和基准。

Abstract: Online Temporal Action Localization (On-TAL) aims to detect the occurrence time and category of actions in untrimmed streaming videos immediately upon their completion. Recent advancements in this field focus on developing more sophisticated frameworks, shifting from Online Action Detection (OAD)-based aggregation paradigm to instance-level understanding. However, existing approaches are typically trained on specific domains and often exhibit limited generalization capabilities when applied to arbitrary videos, particularly in the presence of previously unseen actions. In this paper, we introduce a new task called Online Zero-shot Temporal Action Localization (OZ-TAL), which aims to detect previously unseen actions in an online fashion. Furthermore, we propose a training-free framework that leverages off-the-shelf Vision-Language Models (VLMs) while introducing additional mechanisms to enhance visual representations and mitigate their inherent biases. We establish new benchmarks and representative baselines for OZ-TAL on THUMOS14 and ActivityNet-1.3, and extensive experiments demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches under both offline and online zero-shot settings.

</details>


### [165] [INFANiTE: Implicit Neural representation for high-resolution Fetal brain spatio-temporal Atlas learNing from clinical Thick-slicE MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09977)
*Xiaotian Hu,Mingxuan Liu,Hongjia Yang,Juncheng Zhu,Yijin Li,Yifei Chen,Haoxiang Li,Tongxi Song,Zihan Li,Yingqi Hao,Ziyu Li,Yujin Zhang,Gang Ning,Yi Liao,Haibo Qu,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: INFANiTE是一个基于隐式神经表示（INR）的框架，能够直接从临床厚层MRI扫描中学习高分辨率胎儿脑时空图谱，绕过了传统的切片到体积重建和迭代非刚性配准步骤，将处理时间从数天缩短到数小时，并在多项指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胎儿脑图谱构建流程需要数天时间进行切片到体积重建（SVR）和迭代体积配准，导致大规模队列的图谱构建不切实际。

Method: 提出INFANiTE框架，利用隐式神经表示（INR）直接从临床厚层MRI中学习高分辨率胎儿脑时空图谱，完全绕开SVR和迭代非刚性配准步骤。

Result: INFANiTE在主体一致性、参考保真度、内在质量和生物学合理性方面优于现有基线，即使在稀疏数据条件下也表现良好；端到端处理时间从数天缩短到数小时。

Conclusion: INFANiTE显著加速了胎儿脑图谱构建，促进大规模人群水平的胎儿脑分析，且代码已公开。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal fetal brain atlases are important for characterizing normative neurodevelopment and identifying congenital anomalies. However, existing atlas construction pipelines necessitate days for slice-to-volume reconstruction (SVR) to generate high-resolution 3D brain volumes and several additional days for iterative volume registration, thereby rendering atlas construction from large-scale cohorts prohibitively impractical. We address these limitations with INFANiTE, an Implicit Neural Representation (INR) framework for high-resolution Fetal brain spatio-temporal Atlas learNing from clinical Thick-slicE MRI scans, bypassing both the costly SVR and the iterative non-rigid registration steps entirely, thereby substantially accelerating atlas construction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that INFANiTE outperforms existing baselines in subject consistency, reference fidelity, intrinsic quality and biological plausibility, even under challenging sparse-data settings. Additionally, INFANiTE reduces the end-to-end processing time (i.e., from raw scans to the final atlas) from days to hours compared to the traditional 3D volume-based pipeline (e.g., SyGN), facilitating large-scale population-level fetal brain analysis. Our code is publicly available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/INFANiTE-5D74

</details>


### [166] [ERASE: Eliminating Redundant Visual Tokens via Adaptive Two-Stage Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09982)
*Yuna Lee,Kyoungho Min,Yulhwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ERASE框架，一种两阶段视觉标记剪枝方法，通过自适应图像复杂度的策略显著减少视觉标记数量，在85%剪枝率下仍能保持89.46%的原始模型准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在处理高分辨率图像时产生大量视觉标记，导致计算开销巨大。当前剪枝方法依赖模型内部学习到的语义特征来捕捉视觉冗余，且缺乏根据输入图像复杂度动态调整剪枝策略的自适应机制。

Method: 提出ERASE两阶段视觉标记剪枝框架，通过自适应图像复杂度的剪枝策略来识别和保留重要标记。该方法不同于现有依赖语义特征的方法，能够根据输入图像复杂度动态调整剪枝策略。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型上，当视觉标记剪枝率达到85%时，ERASE保留了89.46%的原始模型准确率，而最佳已有方法仅保留78.1%。

Conclusion: ERASE能够在大幅减少视觉标记数量的同时有效保持模型准确率，显著优于现有方法，为高分辨率视觉语言模型的高效推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable large language models (LLMs) to process high-resolution images, significantly improving real-world multimodal understanding. However, this capability introduces a large number of vision tokens, resulting in substantial computational overhead. To mitigate this issue, various vision token pruning methods have been proposed. Nevertheless, existing approaches predominantly rely on learned semantic features within the model to capture visual redundancy. Moreover, they lack adaptive mechanisms to adjust pruning strategies according to the complexity of the input image. In this paper, we propose ERASE, a two-stage vision token pruning framework that identifies and retains salient tokens through pruning strategies adaptive to image complexity. Experiment results demonstrate that ERASE significantly reduces vision tokens while preserving accuracy. For Qwen2.5-VL-7B, at a token pruning ratio of 85\%, ERASE retains 89.46% of the original model accuracy, whereas the best prior method retains only 78.1%. Our code is available at https://github.com/Tuna-Luna/ERASE.

</details>


### [167] [Geometric 4D Stitching for Grounded 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09984)
*Sunwoo Park,Taesung Kwon,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Geometric 4D Stitching框架，通过显式识别并补全缺失几何区域，在10分钟内完成4D场景重建，提升几何一致性并支持交互式扩展与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D生成方法存在几何不一致性问题，且基于辐射场的重建需要昂贵优化，辐射表示会将几何不一致吸收到视角依赖特性中，无法保证几何一致性。

Method: 提出Geometric 4D Stitching框架，显式识别缺失几何区域，用几何上可靠的4D stitches进行补全，在单张RTX 5090 GPU上每步场景扩展仅需10分钟。

Result: 构建的4D场景表示几何一致性更好，重建时间短（10分钟内），支持4D网格的迭代扩展和4D场景编辑。

Conclusion: 显式4D stitching方法有效解决了几何不一致问题，且具有高效性、可扩展性和可编辑性。

Abstract: Recent 4D generation methods complete scene-level missing information using generative models and reconstruct the scene into radiance-based representations. However, these pipelines often present geometric inconsistencies in the generated content, and the radiance-based reconstruction requires expensive optimization. Furthermore, radiance-based representations often absorb these geometric inconsistencies into their view-dependent nature, failing to enforce the grounded geometric consistency. To address these issues, we propose Geometric 4D Stitching, an efficient framework that explicitly identifies missing geometric regions and complements them with geometrically grounded 4D stitches. As a result, our method constructs 4D scene representations in under 10 minutes on a single NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU per one-step scene expansion, while improving geometric consistency. Moreover, we demonstrate that our explicit 4D stitching supports interative expansion of 4D mesh as well as 4D scene editing.

</details>


### [168] [Omni-Persona: Systematic Benchmarking and Improving Omnimodal Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09996)
*Yeongtak Oh,Dongwook Lee,Sangkwon Park,Heeseung Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Omni-Persona，首个覆盖文本、图像和音频的全模态个性化基准，通过Persona模态图形式化跨模态路由任务，并引入校准准确率(Cal)来统一评估带缺失人物场景的接地行为，实验揭示了开源模型在音频与视觉接地上的差距、参数规模与校准的分离性，以及SFT与RLVR在个性化任务中的不同表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大模型在个性化研究上主要局限于视觉-语言领域，缺乏同时覆盖文本、图像和音频的统一全模态基准，并且缺少对缺失人物场景的系统性接地行为研究方法论。

Method: 构建Omni-Persona基准，包含4个任务组和18个细粒度任务（约750个条目），形式化为Persona模态图上的跨模态路由任务；提出校准准确率(Cal)指标，联合奖励正确接地和适当弃权，将缺失人物查询纳入统一评估框架。

Result: 三个诊断发现：(i)开源模型存在音频与视觉接地的差距，RLVR通过密集规则监督部分弥合；(ii)可答召回率和参数规模无法完全诊断模型表现，强召回可与缺失人物幻觉共存，大模型不一定获得更高Cal；(iii)SFT受限于大规模标注数据构建难度，RLVR通过结果级可验证反馈更一致地泛化，但在当前奖励设计下向保守行为漂移并降低生成质量。

Conclusion: Omni-Persona作为一个诊断框架，揭示了全模态个性化中的陷阱，为未来的后训练和奖励设计提供指导。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models have advanced across text, image, and audio, personalization research has remained primarily vision-language, with unified omnimodal benchmarking that jointly covers text, image, and audio still limited, and lacking the methodological rigor to account for absent-persona scenarios or systematic grounding studies. We introduce Omni-Persona, the first comprehensive benchmark for omnimodal personalization. We formalize the task as cross-modal routing over the \emph{Persona Modality Graph}, encompassing 4 task groups and 18 fine-grained tasks across ${\sim}750$ items. To rigorously diagnose grounding behavior, we propose \emph{Calibrated Accuracy ($\mathrm{Cal}$)}, which jointly rewards correct grounding and appropriate abstention, incorporating absent-persona queries within a unified evaluation framework. On our dedicated experiments, three diagnostic findings emerge: (i) open-source models show a consistent audio-vs-visual grounding gap that RLVR partially narrows via dense rule-based supervision; (ii) answerable recall and parameter scale are incomplete diagnostics, since strong recall can coexist with absent-persona hallucination and larger models do not always achieve higher $\mathrm{Cal}$, exposing calibration as a separate evaluation axis; and (iii) SFT is bounded by the difficulty of constructing annotated ground-truth supervision at scale, while RLVR generalizes more consistently through outcome-level verifiable feedback yet drifts toward conservative behavior and lower generation quality under our reward design. Omni-Persona thus serves as a diagnostic framework that surfaces the pitfalls of omnimodal personalization, guiding future post-training and reward design.

</details>


### [169] [Med-StepBench: A Hierarchical Reasoning Framework for Evaluating Hallucinations in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10002)
*Minh Khoi Nguyen,Dai Lam Le,Amir Reza Jafari,Tuan Dung Nguyen,Mai Hong Son,Mai Huy Thong,Quang Huy Nguyen,Thanh Trung Nguyen,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi,Phi Le Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Med-StepBench是首个面向3D肿瘤PET/CT影像的逐步幻觉检测基准，包含12,000+图像和100万+图像-陈述对，揭示了当前VLM在临床分步推理中存在的系统性缺陷及对对抗性解释的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗幻觉基准仅关注2D影像的单次诊断问题，无法检测基于正确定位和异常识别的逐步推理错误，导致看似正确的诊断背后隐藏关键推理漏洞。

Method: 构建包含12,000+图像和100万+图像-陈述对的大规模基准，将临床推理分解为四个专家设计的诊断阶段，支持体素和多视角2D数据，并对通用与医学VLM进行逐步骤评估。

Result: 通用与医学VLM在逐步评估中表现出系统性失败模式，且对临床合理的对抗性中间解释高度脆弱，即使存在矛盾视觉证据仍会显著放大幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在多步临床推理的 grounding 方面存在根本性局限，Med-StepBench可作为开发更安全、可靠医疗VLM的严格基准。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong performance in medical image understanding, but frequently generate clinically plausible yet incorrect statements, raising significant safety concerns. Existing medical hallucination benchmarks primarily focus on 2D imaging with one-shot diagnostic questions, offering limited insight into whether predictions are grounded in correct localization and abnormality identification, allowing critical reasoning errors to remain hidden behind seemingly correct diagnoses. We introduce Med-StepBench, the first large-scale benchmark for step-wise hallucination detection in 3D oncological PET/CT, comprising over 12,000 images and more than 1,000,000 image-statement pairs across volumetric and multi-view 2D data, which decomposes clinical reasoning into four expert-designed diagnostic stages. Using clinician-verified annotations, we perform the first step-level evaluation of general-purpose and medical VLMs, revealing systematic failure modes obscured by aggregate accuracy metrics. Furthermore, we show that current VLMs are highly susceptible to adversarial yet clinically plausible intermediate explanations, which significantly amplify hallucinations despite contradictory visual evidence. Together, our findings highlight fundamental limitations in grounding multi-step clinical reasoning and establish Med-StepBench as a rigorous benchmark for developing safer and more reliable medical VLMs.

</details>


### [170] [MUSDA: Multi-source Multi-modality Unsupervised Domain Adaptive 3D Object Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10026)
*Xiaohu Lu,Hamed Khatounabadi,Hayder Radha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶多源多模态无监督域适应3D目标检测框架，通过分层空间条件域分类器对齐跨模态特征，并利用原型图加权融合策略聚合多源域预测，在Waymo、nuScenes和Lyft数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域适应方法通常局限于单一源域或单一模态，难以有效处理多源、多模态场景下的3D目标检测任务，而自动驾驶领域已有大量带标注的多模态数据集可用于提升模型在新环境中的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 提出分层空间条件（HSC）域分类器，在两层粒度上联合对齐相机和LiDAR模态的特征；2) 构建每对域之间的原型图；3) 基于原型图加权（PGW）策略，融合多个源域检测头的预测结果。

Result: 在Waymo、nuScenes和Lyft三个主流3D目标检测数据集上的实验表明，该框架有效整合了多模态和多源域信息，一致性地优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过分层跨模态对齐与原型图引导的多源融合策略，能够显著提升无监督域适应在自动驾驶3D目标检测中的性能，为充分利用已有的多源标注数据提供了有效途径。

Abstract: With the advancement of autonomous driving, numerous annotated multi-modality datasets have become available. This presents an opportunity to develop domain-adaptive 3D object detectors for new environments without relying on labor-intensive manual annotations. However, traditional domain adaptation methods typically focus on a single source domain or a single modality, limiting their effectiveness in multi-source, multi-modality scenarios. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multi-source, multi-modality unsupervised domain adaptation in 3D object detection for autonomous driving. Given multiple labeled source domains and one unlabeled target domain, our framework first introduces hierarchical spatially-conditioned (HSC) domain classifiers, which jointly align features from both camera and LiDAR modalities at two distinct levels for each source-target domain pair. To effectively leverage information from multiple source domains, we construct a prototype graph between each pair of domains. Based on this, we develop a prototype graph weighted (PGW) multi-source fusion strategy to aggregate predictions from multiple source detection heads. Experimental results on three widely used 3D object detection datasets - Waymo, nuScenes, and Lyft - demonstrate that our proposed framework effectively integrates information across both modalities and source domains, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [171] [Slum Detection and Density Mapping with AlphaEarth Foundations: A Representation Learning Evaluation Across 12 Global Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10029)
*Shuyang Hou,Ziqi Liu,Haoyue Jiao,Zhangyan Xu,Xiaopu Zhang,Lutong Xie,Yaxian Qing,Jianyuan Liang,Xuefeng Guan,Huayi Wua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) 10米分辨率地表嵌入在贫民窟分类和亚像素密度估计中的表现，实验覆盖12个城市69个城市-年对(2017-2024)。主要发现包括：同城跨年训练最优（中位空间F1=0.616, R²=0.466），回归任务受限于零/非零边界判别（正像素R²均为负），分类在k=32饱和而回归在k=64未饱和，POI特征带来最大密度增益（ΔR²=+0.064），全AOI推理保持良好结构一致性（平均SSIM=0.926）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级贫民窟制图长期面临跨城市泛化能力差、缺乏连续密度估计以及全球可比性弱的三大瓶颈。AEF作为全球一致的64维年度地表嵌入提供了新的分析基础，但其在贫民窟检测这一间接耦合任务中的适用性尚未被验证。

Method: 使用GRAM伪掩码作为监督标签，在12个城市69个城市-年对上评估AEF嵌入。实验涵盖4种训练策略、2种协议（随机分割和3×3空间块交叉验证）、6种辅助特征配置和5种基线模型，并辅以PCA、SHAP等表征分析及全AOI制图。

Result: (1)同城跨年训练最优；(2)回归R²主要由零/非零边界判别驱动，正像素R²均为负；(3)PC36双任务均排名最高，分类在k=32饱和，回归在k=64未饱和；(4)POI特征增益最大；(5)全AOI推理保持良好结构（SSIM=0.926）。

Conclusion: 该研究清晰界定了基础模型嵌入在贫民窟监测中的能力边界及其所需的互补需求。

Abstract: Pixel-level slum mapping has long been constrained by limited cross-city generalisation, the absence of continuous density estimation, and weak global comparability. AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF), a globally consistent 64-dimensional annual surface embedding at 10 m, offers a new analysis-ready basis for lightweight slum monitoring, but its applicability to slum detection - an indirectly coupled task shaped by both built form and socio-economic processes - remains untested. We evaluate AEF on slum classification and sub-pixel density estimation across 12 cities and 69 city-year pairs (2017-2024), using GRAM pseudo-masks as supervisory labels. The evaluation spans four training strategies, two protocols (random split and 3x3 spatial block cross-validation), six auxiliary feature configurations, and five baseline models, complemented by representation-level analyses (PCA, SHAP) and full-AOI mapping. Five findings emerge. (1) Same-city cross-year training is optimal under both protocols (median spatial F1 = 0.616, R^2 = 0.466); temporal expansion outperforms cross-city transfer, indicating city-scale representational drift. (2) Regression R^2 is driven primarily by zero/non-zero boundary discrimination: positive-pixel R^2 is consistently negative across all cities, revealing limited capacity to model intra-pixel density gradients at 10 m. (3) PC36 is consistently top-ranked across tasks; classification saturates at k = 32 while regression remains unsaturated at k = 64. (4) POI features yield the largest density gain (Delta R^2 = +0.064). (5) For six cities meeting dual-task usability thresholds, full-AOI inference across 2017-2024 preserves slum cluster structure (mean SSIM = 0.926). The study delineates the capabilities and complementarity needs of foundation-model embeddings for slum monitoring.

</details>


### [172] [Only Train Once: Uncertainty-Aware One-Class Learning for Face Authenticity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10040)
*Qingchao Jiang,Zhenxuan Hou,Zhiying Zhu,Zhenxing Qian,Xinpeng Zhang,Zaiwang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FADNet是一个基于单类分类的人脸伪造检测框架，仅使用真实人脸数据训练，通过证据深度学习和伪伪造图像生成器提升检测性能，在DF40和ASFD基准上达到96.63%准确率和98.83%精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸伪造检测方法大多是监督二分类，面对未见过的生成范式时性能下降严重，且无法统一检测DeepFakes和完全合成的人脸。

Method: 将伪造检测重构为单类分类任务，仅用真实人脸数据训练；引入证据深度学习量化不确定性；设计即插即用的伪伪造图像生成器来收紧决策边界。

Result: 在DF40和ASFD基准上，平均准确率96.63%，平均精确率98.83%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FADNet通过单类分类和不确定性量化，实现了对多种生成范式的高泛化能力，是一种通用的人脸伪造检测框架。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative paradigms has enabled the creation of highly realistic imagery, which escalating the risks of identity fraud and the dissemination of disinformation. Most existing approaches frame face forgery detection as a fully supervised binary classification problem. Consequently, these models typically exhibit significant performance decay when tasked with detecting forgeries from previously unseen generative paradigms. Furthermore, these methods focus exclusively on either DeepFakes or fully synthesized faces, thereby failing to provide a generalized framework for universal face forgery detection. In this paper, we address this challenge by introducing FADNet (Face Authenticity Detector Net),
  % a self-supervised framework that
  which reformulates face forgery detection as a one-class classification (OCC) task. By training exclusively on authentic facial data to capture their intrinsic representations, FADNet flags any image whose feature embedding deviates significantly from the learned distribution of real faces as a forgery. The framework incorporates Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) to quantify predictive uncertainty and utilizes a plug-and-play pseudo-forgery image generator (PFIG) to tighten decision boundaries around authentic data. Extensive experimental evaluations on the DF40 and ASFD benchmarks demonstrate that FADNet achieves superior performance and generalization capabilities. Specifically, FADNet substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, yielding a remarkable average accuracy of 96.63\% and an average precision of 98.83\%.

</details>


### [173] [ExtraVAR: Stage-Aware RoPE Remapping for Resolution Extrapolation in Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10045)
*Feihong Yan,Shaoyu Liu,Haixuan Wang,Shuai Lu,Linfeng Zhang,Huiqi Li,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对VAR模型在图像生成中无法直接进行高分辨率外推的问题，分析了三种失败模式（全局重复、局部重复、细节退化）并归因于频带-阶段不匹配，提出了阶段感知的RoPE重映射策略和熵驱动的自适应注意力校准方法，显著提升了高分辨率图像生成的结构一致性和细节保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR模型在固定分辨率下训练，无法直接生成更高分辨率的图像；直接将LLM或扩散模型的零训练外推方法迁移到VAR会导致三种典型的失败模式，亟需一种针对VAR特性的分辨率外推方法。

Method: 1) 阶段感知的RoPE重映射（Stage-Aware RoPE Remapping）：分析发现VAR的粗到细生成过程中每个阶段由不同的RoPE频带主导，针对每个频带设计阶段特定的重映射规则，联合抑制三种失败模式。2) 熵驱动的自适应注意力校准（Entropy-Driven Adaptive Attention Calibration）：提出分辨率不变的归一化熵来量化注意力分散程度，并推导出闭式逐头缩放因子，将外推分辨率的注意力熵对齐到训练分辨率的水平。

Result: 在结构一致性和细节保真度上，该方法一致性地超越了先前的分辨率外推方法。

Conclusion: 通过频带-阶段匹配分析和注意力熵校准，实现了VAR模型在更高分辨率下的高效、高质量图像生成，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) models have emerged as a strong alternative to diffusion for image synthesis, yet their fixed training resolution prevents direct generation at higher resolutions. Naively transferring training-free extrapolation methods from LLMs or diffusion models to VAR yields three characteristic failure modes: global repetition, local repetition, and detail degradation. We trace them to a unified band-stage mismatch: VAR generates images in a coarse-to-fine, scale-wise process where each stage is driven by a distinct dominant RoPE frequency band, and each failure mode emerges when the dominant band of a particular stage is disrupted. Building on this insight, we propose Stage-Aware RoPE Remapping, a training-free strategy that assigns each frequency band a stage-specific remapping rule, jointly suppressing all three failure modes. We further observe that attention becomes systematically dispersed as the image resolution increases. Existing methods typically depend on predefined attention scaling factors, which are neither adaptive to the target resolution nor capable of faithfully capturing the actual extent of attention dispersion. We therefore propose Entropy-Driven Adaptive Attention Calibration, which quantifies dispersion via a resolution-invariant normalized entropy and yields a closed-form per-head scaling factor that realigns the extrapolated-resolution attention entropy with its training-resolution counterpart. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms prior resolution-extrapolation methods in both structural coherence and fine-detail fidelity. Our code is available at https://github.com/feihongyan1/ExtraVAR.

</details>


### [174] [PixelFlowCast: Latent-Free Precipitation Nowcasting via Pixel Mean Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10046)
*Yufeng Zhu,Chunlei Shi,Yongchao Feng,Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelFlowCast是一种两阶段概率预测框架，无需潜在压缩即可实现高效高保真的降水临近预报，在SEVIR数据集上优于主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型推理速度慢，条件流匹配虽提升效率但依赖潜在空间压缩导致高频细节丢失，限制了精细化预测质量。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段确定性模型生成粗粒度预测捕捉全局演变趋势；第二阶段KANCondNet提取深度时空演化特征，结合无潜在空间的少步Pixel Mean Flows (PMF)预测器，采用x-预测机制生成高质量预测。

Result: 在SEVIR公开数据集上，PixelFlowCast在预测精度和推理效率上均优于现有主流方法，尤其在长序列预测中表现突出。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持快速推理的同时有效保留细粒度物理细节，具备实际业务部署的潜力。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting aims to forecast short-term radar echo sequences for extreme weather warning, where both prediction fidelity and inference efficiency are critical for real-world deployment. However, diffusion-based models, despite their strong generative capability, suffer from slow inference due to multi-step sampling trajectories, limiting their practical usability. Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) improves efficiency via straightened trajectories, but relies on latent space compression, which inevitably discards high-frequency physical details and degrades fine-grained prediction quality. To address these limitations, we propose PixelFlowCast, a two-stage probabilistic forecasting framework that achieves both high-efficiency and high-fidelity prediction without latent compression. Specifically, in the first stage, a deterministic model first produces coarse forecasts to capture global evolution trends. In the subsequent stage, the proposed KANCondNet extracts deep spatiotemporal evolution features to provide accurate conditional guidance. Based on this, a latent-free, few-step Pixel Mean Flows (PMF) predictor employs an $x$-prediction mechanism to generate high-quality predictions, effectively preserving fine-grained structures while maintaining fast inference. Experiments on the publicly available SEVIR dataset demonstrate that PixelFlowCast outperforms existing mainstream methods in both prediction accuracy and inference efficiency, particularly for long sequence forecasting, highlighting its strong potential for real-world operational deployment.

</details>


### [175] [EchoPrune: Interpreting Redundancy as Temporal Echoes for Efficient VideoLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10050)
*Jiameng Li,Minye Wu,Jiezhang Cao,Aleksei Tiulpin,Matthew B. Blaschko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoPrune是一种轻量级、无需训练的令牌剪枝方法，通过将冗余视频令牌视为"时间回声"，基于查询引导的跨模态相关性和时间重建误差进行评分剪枝，使VideoLLMs在固定令牌预算下可处理多达20倍的视频帧，性能提升+8.6%，推理速度提升5.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中，密集帧采样产生大量视觉令牌，稀疏采样又会遗漏关键时序证据导致幻觉，现有训练无关的令牌缩减方法要么将视频等同于静态图像，要么依赖片段级合并启发式，削弱了细粒度时空建模并引入额外开销。

Method: 提出EchoPrune方法，核心思想是将冗余视频令牌解释为"时间回声"：若某令牌能从上一帧良好重建，则为冗余回声；否则可能捕捉新事件、运动或查询相关视觉证据。通过查询引导的跨模态相关性和时间重建误差（基于连续帧间的对应匹配和回声匹配）对视觉令牌评分，保留任务相关线索和时间新颖性，抑制可预测冗余。

Result: 在LLaVA-OV、Qwen2.5VL和Qwen3VL上，跨六个视频理解基准测试，EchoPrune使VideoLLMs在相同令牌预算下处理多达20倍帧数，Qwen2.5VL-7B上性能提升+8.6%，预填充推理速度提升5.6倍。

Conclusion: EchoPrune有效解决了长视频理解中令牌冗余与关键信息保留之间的权衡问题，通过创新的"时间回声"视角实现高效的训练无关令牌剪枝，在提升性能的同时显著加速推理。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding remains challenging for Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs), as the dense frame sampling introduces massive visual tokens while sparse sampling risks missing critical temporal evidence and leading to LLM hallucination. Existing training-free token reduction methods either treat videos equally as static images or rely on segment-level merging heuristics, which weaken fine-grained spatiotemporal modeling and introduce additional overhead. In this paper, we propose EchoPrune, a lightweight and training-free token pruning method that improves temporal resolution under a fixed LLM-side visual token budget. Our core idea is to interpret redundant video tokens as temporal echoes: if a token is well reconstructed from the previous frame, it is merely a temporally redundant echo; otherwise, it may capture new events, motion, or query-relevant visual evidence. Based on this insight, EchoPrune scores visual tokens by (i) query-guided crossmodal relevance and (ii) temporal reconstruction error, measured by correspondence matching and echo matching across consecutive frames. The selected tokens preserve task-relevant cues and temporal novelty while suppressing predictable redundancy, allowing VideoLLMs to observe more frames without increasing the decoding budget. Extensive experiments on LLaVA-OV, Qwen2.5VL, and Qwen3VL across six video understanding benchmarks show that EchoPrune enables VideoLLMs to process up to 20x frames under the same token budget, yielding improved performance (+8.6%) and inference speedup (5.6x for prefilling) on Qwen2.5VL-7B.

</details>


### [176] [MFVLR: Multi-domain Fine-grained Vision-Language Reconstruction for Generalizable Diffusion Face Forgery Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10071)
*Yaning Zhang,Tianyi Wang,Zan Gao,Yibo Zhao,Chunjie Ma,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多域细粒度视觉-语言重建模型（MFVLR），通过语言引导的伪造表征学习，实现可泛化的扩散合成人脸伪造检测与定位，在跨生成器、跨伪造类型和跨数据集评估中均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅利用图像模态，未充分探索细粒度文本等模态信息，且主要针对GAN生成的人脸，难以检测和定位扩散模型合成的人脸，泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出MFVLR模型：1）设计细粒度语言Transformer通过语言重建学习通用语言嵌入；2）设计多域视觉编码器从图像域和残差域捕获视觉伪造模式；3）设计视觉解码器重建图像外观并实现伪造定位；4）提出即插即用的视觉注入模块增强视觉与语言嵌入的交互。

Result: 在跨生成器、跨伪造类型和跨数据集等多种设置下，MFVLR均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 语言引导的多域视觉-语言重建方法能有效提升扩散合成人脸伪造检测与定位的泛化能力。

Abstract: The swift advancement in photo-realistic face generation technology has sparked considerable concerns across society and academia, emphasizing the requirement of generalizable face forgery detection and localization methods. Prior works tend to capture face forgery patterns across multiple domains using image modality, other modalities like fine-grained texts are not comprehensively investigated, which restricts the generalization capability of models. Besides, they usually analyze facial images created by GAN, but struggle to identify and localize those synthesized by diffusion. To solve the problems, in this paper, we devise a novel multi-domain fine-grained vision-language reconstruction (MFVLR) model, which explores comprehensive and diverse visual forgery traces via language-guided face forgery representation learning, to achieve generalizable diffusion-synthesized face forgery detection and localization (DFFDL). Specifically, we devise a fine-grained language transformer that studies general fine-grained language embeddings using language reconstruction. We propose a multi-domain vision encoder to capture general and complementary visual forgery patterns across the image and residual domains. A vision decoder is designed to reconstruct image appearance and achieve forgery localization. Besides, we propose an innovative plug-and-play vision injection module to enhance the interaction between the vision and language embeddings. Extensive experiments and visualizations demonstrate that our network outperforms the state of the art on different settings like cross-generator, cross-forgery, and cross-dataset evaluations.

</details>


### [177] [SocialDirector: Training-Free Social Interaction Control for Multi-Person Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10079)
*Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Caixin Kang,Yifei Huang,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SocialDirector是一种无需训练的视频交互控制器，通过调制交叉注意力图，解决多人物视频生成中角色-动作不匹配、社交动态混乱和动作目标错误问题，显著提升社交交互的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型无法对多人社交交互（如对话、手势、协调动作）进行显式控制，常出现执行者与动作不匹配、社交动态混乱、动作目标错误等问题。

Method: 提出SocialDirector，包含两个模块：1) Social Actor Masking，通过时空掩码约束每个人的视觉token只关注其对应的文本描述；2) Directional Reweighting，增强方向性词汇的注意力权重，引导动作指向正确目标。无需训练，直接调制交叉注意力图。

Result: 在多种视频生成模型上的实验表明，SocialDirector显著提升了交互保真度，接近真实视频的上限水平。

Conclusion: SocialDirector通过无需训练的注意力调控方法，有效解决了多人社交视频生成中的角色-动作对应和动作指向问题，为可控社交交互视频生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Video generation has advanced rapidly, producing photorealistic videos from text or image prompts. Meanwhile, film production and social robotics increasingly demand multi-person videos with rich social interactions, including conversations, gestures, and coordinated actions. However, existing models offer no explicit control over interactions, such as who performs which action, when it occurs, and toward whom it is directed. This often results in wrong person performing unintended actions (actor-action mismatch), disordered social dynamics, and wrong action targets. To address these challenges, we present SocialDirector, a training-free interaction controller that enhances the generation model by modulating cross-attention maps. SocialDirector contains two modules: Social Actor Masking and Directional Reweighting. Social Actor Masking constrains each person's visual tokens to attend only to their own textual descriptions via a spatiotemporal mask, avoiding actor-action mismatch and disordered social dynamics. Directional Reweighting amplifies attention to directional words (e.g., "leftward", "right"), leading each action towards its intended target. To evaluate generated social interactions, we annotate existing datasets with interaction descriptions and build a fully automated evaluation pipeline powered by open-source VLMs. Experiments on different video generation models show that SocialDirector significantly improves interaction fidelity and approaches the upper bound set by real videos.

</details>


### [178] [Initiation of Interaction Detection Framework using a Nonverbal Cue for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10087)
*Guhnoo Yun,Juhan Yoo,Kijung Kim,Dong Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于音视觉传感器融合的无关键词人机交互（HRI）起始检测（IoI）框架，用于家庭环境中。机器人通过自身传感器和外部视觉传感器进行人体检测与跟踪，结合声源定位和面部朝向判断用户是否发起交互，并设计了状态转移模型，在ROS环境下实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的HRI交互起始检测依赖特定关键词唤醒，缺乏自然性。本文旨在利用多模态感知（音频+视觉）实现无关键词的自然交互起始检测，提升人机交互的流畅性和用户体验。

Method: 提出IoI检测框架：1）机器人使用自身音频/视觉传感器及外部视觉传感器进行人体检测与跟踪；2）通过声源定位获取用户位置，结合人脸朝向判定是否面向机器人；3）若用户说话且面向机器人，则检测为交互起始；4）若用户不说话但长时间注视机器人，也视为交互起始；5）设计状态转移模型，在ROS环境下集成实现。

Result: 通过移动机器人实验验证了所提出的IoI检测框架和状态转移模型的有效性，系统能够在家庭环境中实现无关键词的交互起始检测。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了无需关键词的人机交互起始检测系统，融合音频与视觉信息，利用状态转移模型提升了交互检测的自然性和鲁棒性，适用于家庭服务机器人场景。

Abstract: This paper describes an initiation of interaction(IoI) detection framework without keywords for human-robot interaction(HRI) based on audio and vision sensor fusion in a domestic environment. In the proposed framework, the robot has its own audio and vision sensors, and can employ external vision sensor for stable human detection and tracking. When the user starts to speak while looking at the robot, the robot can localize his or her position by its sound source localization together with human tracking information. Then the robot can detect the IoI if it perceives the face of the speaker faces the robot. In case that the user does not speak directly, the robot can also detect the IoI if he or she looks at the robot for more than predefined periods of time. A state transition model for the proposed IoI detection framework is designed and verified by experiments with a mobile robot. In order to implement and associate our model in a robot architecture, all the components are implemented and integrated in the Robot Operating System(ROS) environment.

</details>


### [179] [ViSRA: A Video-based Spatial Reasoning Agent for Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10106)
*Tingshu Mou,Jiabo He,Renying Wang,Ce Liu,Hao Yang,Tiehua Zhang,Jingjing Chen,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViSRA是一个无需训练的视频空间推理框架，通过集成专家模型的显式空间信息，以即插即用方式增强多模态大语言模型的空间推理能力，在多个基准上取得最高28.9%的绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在3D空间智能方面的进展主要依赖后训练和人工标注数据，而推理阶段的方法相对未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出ViSRA（基于视频的空间推理智能体），采用无需训练的方式，通过利用专家模型提供的显式空间信息，以模块化、可扩展的方式激发MLLM的空间推理能力，实现即插即用的灵活范式。

Result: 在多个MLLM上进行实验，在现有基准上取得最高15.6%的绝对提升，在未见过的3D空间推理任务上取得最高28.9%的绝对提升，且表现出一致的改进。

Conclusion: ViSRA实现了人类对齐且可迁移的3D理解，避免了任务特定的过拟合，同时无需后训练计算成本和繁重的人工标注。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) target 3D spatial intelligence, yet the progress has been largely driven by post-training on curated benchmarks, leaving the inference-time approach relatively underexplored. In this paper, we take a training-free perspective and introduce ViSRA, a human-aligned Video-based Spatial Reasoning Agent, as a framework to probe the spatial reasoning mechanism of MLLMs. ViSRA elicits spatial reasoning in a modular and extensible manner by leveraging explicit spatial information from expert models, enabling a plug-and-play flexible paradigm. ViSRA offers two key advantages: (1) human-aligned and transferable 3D understanding rather than task-specific overfitting; and (2) no post-training computational cost along with heavy manual curation of spatial reasoning datasets. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvement across a set of MLLMs on both existing benchmarks and unseen 3D spatial reasoning tasks, with ViSRA outperforming baselines by up to a 15.6% and 28.9% absolute margin respectively.

</details>


### [180] [MicroWorld: Empowering Multimodal Large Language Models to Bridge the Microscopic Domain Gap with Multimodal Attribute Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10120)
*Manyu Li,Ruian He,Chenxi Ma,Weimin Tan,Bo Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroWorld框架，通过构建多模态属性属性图（MAPG）来增强多模态大模型（MLLMs）在显微镜科学推理中的表现，无需领域微调，在MicroVQA基准上提升37.5%，超越GPT-5达13.0%，实现新最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在科学推理中潜力巨大，但在显微镜等专业领域受限于领域训练数据稀缺以及将细粒度专家知识编码进模型参数的困难。

Method: MicroWorld首先从大规模科学图像-文本语料中提取生物实体和关系（通过scispaCy或LLM三元组挖掘），利用Qwen3-VL-Embedding将图像与实体对齐到共享嵌入空间，构建包含约111K节点和346K条边的知识图谱。推理时，通过图增强检索管道将查询实体匹配到MAPG，并将结构化的知识上下文注入MLLM提示中。

Result: 在MicroVQA基准上，MicroWorld使Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct的推理性能提升37.5%，并以超过GPT-5 13.0%的优势达到新最先进水平。在MicroBench基准上也获得6.0%的性能提升。

Conclusion: MicroWorld通过外部结构化知识在推理时增强MLLM，有效提升了专业领域科学推理的泛化能力，质化案例揭示了结构化知识改善推理的机制及未来改进方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) show remarkable potential for scientific reasoning, yet their performance in specialized domains such as microscopy remains limited by the scarcity of domain-specific training data and the difficulty of encoding fine-grained expert knowledge into model parameters. To bridge the gap, we introduce MicroWorld, a framework that constructs a multimodal attributed property graph (MAPG) from large-scale scientific image--caption corpora and leverages it to augment MLLM reasoning at inference time without any domain-specific fine-tuning. MicroWorld extracts biomedical entities and relations via scispaCy or LLM-based triplet mining, aligns images and entities in a shared embedding space using Qwen3-VL-Embedding, and assembles a knowledge graph comprising approximately 111K nodes and 346K typed edges spanning eight relation categories. At inference time, a graph-augmented retrieval pipeline matches query entities to the MAPG and injects structured knowledge context into the MLLM prompt. On the MicroVQA benchmark, MicroWorld improves the reasoning performance of Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct by 37.5%, outperforming GPT-5 by 13.0% to achieve a new state-of-the-art. Furthermore, it yields a 6.0% performance gain on the MicroBench benchmark. Extensive experiments demonstrate the enhanced generalization capability introduced by MicroWorld. A qualitative case study further reveals both the mechanisms through which structured knowledge improves reasoning and the failure modes that point to promising future directions. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ieellee/MicroWorld.

</details>


### [181] [Fashion130K: An E-commerce Fashion Dataset for Outfit Generation with Unified Multi-modal Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10127)
*Yu He,Ting Zhu,Yichun Liu,Lichen Ma,Xinyuan Shan,Jingling Fu,Yu Shi,Junshi Huang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出电商数据集Fashion130k和统一多模态条件框架UMC，通过嵌入精炼器和融合Transformer对齐文本与图像提示，实现服装生成的一致性和视觉连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服装生成研究主要利用参考图像和文本提示提升视觉一致性，但缺乏全面的电商数据集以及对多模态条件的充分利用，服装生成的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 构建Fashion130k电商数据集；设计UMC框架，包含嵌入精炼器提取统一多模态嵌入，融合Transformer对齐文本与图像的模态差异，并重新设计生成模型中的注意力机制以增强提示与噪声图像的相关性。

Result: 在真实应用和基准测试上，UMC在视觉一致性方面优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据集和UMC框架为生成模型的多模态提示提供了通用且精细的探索路径，有效提升了服装生成的一致性。

Abstract: Recent research work on fashion outfit generation focuses on promoting visual consistency of garments by leveraging key information from reference image and text prompt. However, the potential of outfit generation remains underexplored, requiring comprehensive e-commercial dataset and elaborative utilization of multi-modal condition. In this paper, we propose a brand-new e-commerce dataset, named Fashion130k, with various occasions, models, and garment types. For the consistent generation of garment, we design a framework with Unified Multi-modal Condition (UMC) to align and integrate the text and visual prompts into generation model. Specifically, we explore an embedding refiner to extract the unified embeddings of multi-modal prompts, within which a Fusion Transformer is proposed to align the multi-modal embeddings by adjusting the modality gap between text and image. Based on unified embeddings, the attention in generation model is redesigned to emphasis the correlations between prompts and noise image, inducing that the noise image can select the pivotal tokens of prompts for consistent outfit generation. Our dataset and proposed framework offer a general and nuanced exploration of multi-modal prompts for generation models. Extensive experiments on real-world applications and benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of UMC in visual consistency, achieving promising result than that of SoTA methods.

</details>


### [182] [Scaling Vision Models Does Not Consistently Improve Localisation-Based Explanation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10142)
*Mateusz Cedro,Marcin Chlebus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了11个计算机视觉模型（ResNet、DenseNet、Vision Transformer系列），发现增大模型规模（深度和参数量）并不会显著提升事后解释（post-hoc explanations）的质量，较小模型在解释定位精度上往往不逊于甚至优于深层模型；预训练虽能提升预测性能，但并未持续改善解释定位分数，且存在高预测性能但定位精度接近零的情况，表明模型选择时需显式评估可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型规模（深度与复杂度）的增大是否能够改善事后解释方法生成解释的质量，以及预测性能与解释质量之间的关系。

Method: 对11个来自ResNet、DenseNet、Vision Transformer系列的计算机视觉模型（涵盖不同深度和复杂度，包括从头训练和预训练），在三个带有真实分割掩码的图像数据集上，使用五种事后可解释性AI方法生成解释，并通过Relevance Rank Accuracy和新提出的Dual-Polarity Precision两个定位指标量化解释与掩码的对齐程度。

Result: 在大多数统计比较中，增加模型深度和参数量并未提升解释质量；较小模型常与深层模型相当甚至更优。预训练虽提升预测性能并增强解释对权重的依赖，但未持续提升定位分数。部分模型预测性能强但定位精度接近零。

Conclusion: 更大模型并不一定能提供更高质量的解释，在安全敏感场景中，模型选择时应显式评估可解释性，而非仅依赖预测性能指标。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are increasingly scaled to improve predictive accuracy, yet it remains unclear whether scale improves the quality of post-hoc explanations. We investigate this relationship by evaluating 11 computer vision models representing increasing levels of depth and complexity within the ResNet, DenseNet, and Vision Transformer families, trained from scratch or pretrained, across three image datasets with ground-truth segmentation masks. For each model, we generate explanations using five post-hoc explainable AI methods and quantify mask alignment using two localisation metrics: Relevance Rank Accuracy (Arras et al., 2022) and the proposed Dual-Polarity Precision, which measures positive attributions inside the class mask and negative attributions outside it. Across datasets and methods, increasing architectural depth and parameter count does not improve explanation quality in most statistical comparisons, and smaller models often match or exceed deeper variants. While pretraining typically improves predictive performance and increases the dependence of explanations on learned weights, it does not consistently increase localisation scores. We also observe scenarios in which models achieve strong predictive performance while localisation precision is near zero, suggesting that performance metrics alone may not indicate whether predictions are based on the annotated regions. These results indicate that larger models do not reliably provide higher-quality explanations, and that explainability should therefore be assessed explicitly during model selection for safety-sensitive deployments.

</details>


### [183] [MicroViTv2: Beyond the FLOPS for Edge Energy-Friendly Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10148)
*Novendra Setyawan,Chi-Chia Sun,Mao-Hsiu Hsu,Wen-Kai Kuo,Jun-Wei Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MicroViTv2 是一个轻量级视觉Transformer，通过重参数化设计（RepEmbed、RepDW）和单深度可分离转置注意力（SDTA），在略微增加FLOPs的情况下提升了准确率（最高0.5%），并在Jetson AGX Orin上实现了快速推理和高能效，超越了MobileViTv2、EdgeNeXt等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer (ViT) 在视觉任务中精度高但计算成本高，难以部署在边缘设备上，因此需要设计兼顾精度与效率的轻量级模型。

Method: 提出MicroViTv2，核心包括：(1) 重参数化补丁嵌入 (RepEmbed)；(2) 重参数化深度可分离卷积混合器 (RepDW) 用于加速推理；(3) 单深度可分离转置注意力 (SDTA) 用于捕获长距离依赖并减少冗余。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和COCO上，MicroViTv2相比MicroViT提升了最高0.5%的准确率，超越MobileViTv2、EdgeNeXt、EfficientViT，同时在Jetson AGX Orin上保持快速推理和高能效。

Conclusion: 硬件感知设计与结构重参数化是实现高精度与低能耗的关键，验证了评估效率不能仅看FLOPs，需考虑实际部署性能。

Abstract: The Vision Transformer (ViT) achieves remarkable accuracy across visual tasks but remains computationally expensive for edge deployment. This paper presents MicroViTv2, a lightweight Vision Transformer optimized for real-device efficiency. Built upon the original MicroViT, the proposed model is designed based on reparameterized design, specifically Reparameterized Patch Embedding (RepEmbed) and Reparameterized Depth-Wise convolution mixer (RepDW) for faster inference, and introduces the Single Depth-Wise Transposed Attention (SDTA) to capture long-range dependencies with minimal redundancy. Despite slightly higher FLOPs, MicroViTv2 improves accuracy up to 0.5% compared to its predecessor and surpassing MobileViTv2, EdgeNeXt, and EfficientViT while maintaining fast inference and high energy efficiency on Jetson AGX Orin. Experiments on ImageNet-1K and COCO demonstrate that hardware-aware design and structural re-parameterization are key to achieving high accuracy and low energy consumption, validating the need to evaluate efficiency beyond FLOPs. Code is available at https://github.com/novendrastywn/MicroViT.

</details>


### [184] [Improving Temporal Action Segmentation via Constraint-Aware Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10149)
*Yeo Keat Ee,Debaditya Roy,Chen Li,Hao Zhang,Basura Fernando*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级、基于约束的优化框架，通过整合从标注数据中提取的统计结构先验（如转移置信度、动作边界集、每类时长），使用改进的维特比解码算法在推理时优化时序动作分割预测，无需重新训练或增加模型复杂度，在完全和半监督设置下均能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序动作分割方法面临动作变异性大、边界模糊、标注成本高等挑战，基于语法的方法虽有结构先验但依赖复杂解析、可扩展性差，因此需要一种轻量级、无需重新训练即可提升分割质量的方法。

Method: 提出一种轻量级约束优化框架，从标注数据中提取统计结构先验（转移置信度、动作边界集、每类时长），将其集成到改进的维特比解码算法中，在推理阶段直接优化分割预测结果。

Result: 该方法在完全监督和半监督的时序动作分割模型上均能提升性能，有效纠正结构预测错误，且保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: 该轻量级约束优化框架在不增加模型复杂度、无需重新训练的情况下，通过推理时引入统计结构先验，有效提升了时序动作分割的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Temporal action segmentation (TAS) divides untrimmed videos into labeled action segments. While fully supervised methods have advanced the field, challenges such as action variability, ambiguous boundaries, and high annotation costs remain, especially in new or low-resource domains. Grammar-based approaches improve segmentation with structural priors but rely on complex parsing limiting scalability. In this work, we propose a lightweight, constraint-based refinement framework that enhances TAS predictions by integrating statistical structural priors such as transition confidence, action boundary sets, and per-class duration, that can be directly extracted from annotated data. These constraints are integrated into a modified Viterbi decoding algorithm, allowing inference-time refinement without retraining or added model complexity. Our approach improves both fully and semi-supervised TAS models by correcting structural prediction errors while maintaining high efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/LUNAProject22/CAD

</details>


### [185] [Active-SAOOD: Active Sparsely Annotated Oriented Object Detection in Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10162)
*Yu Lin,Jianghang Lin,Kai Ye,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Active-SAOOD方法，通过主动学习在实例级别选择最有价值的稀疏样本来标注，解决了遥感图像中定向目标检测的稀疏标注问题。在仅1%标注比例下，相比基线提升了9%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少遥感图像中定向目标检测的标注成本仍是一大挑战。现有稀疏标注方法存在两个问题：(1)依赖类别相关的采样方式；(2)对稀疏样本特性缺乏深入研究，限制了其发展。

Method: 提出基于主动学习的Active-SAOOD方法。通过模型状态观测模块，在实例级别联合考虑方向、分类和定位的不确定性，以及类内和类间多样性，主动选择最适合当前模型状态的最有价值的稀疏样本进行标注。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，Active-SAOOD在各种随机稀疏标注下显著提升了现有SAOOD方法的性能和稳定性。在仅1%标注比例下，相比基线实现了9%的性能提升。

Conclusion: Active-SAOOD使稀疏标注导向目标检测能够在完全随机初始化的稀疏标注下稳定运行，扩展了其在更广泛真实场景中的适用性，进一步增强了SAOOD在遥感领域的实用价值。

Abstract: Reducing the annotation cost of oriented object detection in remote sensing remains a major challenge. Recently, sparse annotation has gained attention for effectively reducing annotation redundancy in densely remote sensing scenes. However, (1) the sparse data reliance on class-dependent sampling, and (2) the lack of in-depth investigation into the characteristics of sparse samples hinders its further development. This paper proposes an active learning-based sparsely annotated oriented object detection (SAOOD) method, termed Active-SAOOD. Based on a model state observation module, Active-SAOOD actively selects the most valuable sparse samples at the instance level that are best suited to the current model state, by jointly considering orientation, classification, and localization uncertainty, as well as inter- and intra-class diversity. This design enables SAOOD to operate stably under completely randomly initialized sparse annotations and extends its applicability to broader real-world. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that Active-SAOOD significantly improves both performance and stability of existing SAOOD methods under various random sparse annotation. In particular, with only 1\% annotated ratios, it achieves a 9\% performance gain over the baseline, further enhancing the practical value of SAOOD in remote sensing. The code will be public.

</details>


### [186] [Task-Agnostic Noisy Label Detection via Standardized Loss Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10165)
*Inhyuk Park,Doohyun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出标准化损失聚合（SLA）框架，通过重复交叉验证中标准化折叠验证损失的聚合，在样本级别检测医学影像数据集中的噪声标签。SLA将离散硬计数推广为连续估计器，捕捉性能偏差的频率和幅度，在眼底数据集上优于硬计数基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模医学影像数据集中由于观察者间差异和模糊病例导致噪声标签普遍存在，需要一种任务无关、统计稳健的样本级噪声检测方法。

Method: SLA：通过重复交叉验证，将每次折叠的验证损失进行标准化处理，然后聚合这些标准化损失来量化每个样本的标签可靠性，得到连续性的噪声分数。

Result: 在公共眼底数据集上，SLA在所有噪声水平下均优于硬计数基线，尤其在低噪声比下收敛更快，能有效检测出模糊或错误标注的样本。

Conclusion: SLA提供了一种统计稳健、可解释的样本级噪声检测方法，可指导高效重新标注，提升数据集可靠性，适用于任何分类任务。

Abstract: Noisy labels are common in large-scale medical imaging datasets due to inter-observer variability and ambiguous cases. We propose a statistically grounded and task-agnostic framework, Standardized Loss Aggregation (SLA), for detecting noisy labels at the sample level. SLA quantifies label reliability by aggregating standardized fold-level validation losses across repeated cross-validation runs. This formulation generalizes discrete hard-counting schemes into a continuous estimator that captures both the frequency and magnitude of performance deviations, yielding interpretable and statistically stable noisiness scores.
